Remove existing children\n    for child in list(p_elem):\n        p_elem.remove(child)\n\n    run = ET.Element(f\"{{{HP_NS}}}run\", {\"charPrIDRef\": \"0\"})\n    t = ET.SubElement(run, f\"{{{HP_NS}}}t\")\n    t.text = new_text\n    p_elem.append(run)\n\n\ndef remove_linesegarrays(p_elem) -> None:\n    # Quick check appendix presence\n    for p in root.findall(\".//hp:p\", NS):\n        txt = get_paragraph_text(p)\n        if txt == APPENDIX_SENTENCE:\n            appendix_before = txt\n            break\n\n    changed_paragraphs = 0\n\n    # 1) Field replacements based on known labels.\n    for p in root.findall(\".//hp:p\", NS):\n        old_full = get_paragraph_text(p)\n\n        if old_full == APPENDIX_SENTENCE:\n            continue\n\n        new_full = old_full\n\n        # Renewal window line\n        if old_full.startswith(\"갱신 윈도우:\"):\n            new_full = f\"갱신 윈도우: {updates['window_start']} ~ {updates['window_end']}\"\n\n        # Renewal window confirmation line\n        if old_full.startswith(\"갱신 윈도우 확인:\"):\n            new_full = f\"갱신 윈도우 확인: {updates['window_start']} ~ {updates['window_end']}\"\n\n        # Pricing note\n        if old_full.startswith(\"가격 메모:\"):\n            new_full = f\"가격 메모: {updates['note']}\"\n\n        # Follow-ups are handled in a separate pass; skip here.\n\n        if new_full != old_full:\n            clear_and_set_paragraph_text(p, new_full)\n            remove_linesegarrays(p)\n            changed_paragraphs += 1\n\n    # 2) Table cell updates: band and escalation email live in their own paragraphs.\n    # We update by exact old value match, but also by adjacent label if present.\n    for p in root.findall(\".//hp:p\", NS):\n        old_full = get_paragraph_text(p)\n        if old_full == APPENDIX_SENTENCE:\n            continue\n\n        new_full = old_full\n        if old_full.strip() in (\"Standard-18M\", \"Standard-12M\", \"Standard-24M\") or \"-\" in old_full:\n            # We only edit as many paragraphs as we have items; if item count differs, adjust.\n        # For this task data, it is expected to be 3.\n        parent_map = {c: p for p in root.iter() for c in list(p)}\n        first_parent = parent_map.get(followup_ps[0])\n\n        if first_parent is None:\n            raise RuntimeError(\"Could not locate parent for follow-up paragraphs\")\n\n        # Pricing note\n        if old_full.startswith(\"가격 메모:\"):\n            new_full = f\"가격 메모: {updates['note']}\"\n\n        # Follow-ups are handled in a separate pass; skip here.\n\n        if new_full != old_full:\n            clear_and_set_paragraph_text(p, new_full)\n            remove_linesegarrays(p)\n            changed_paragraphs += 1\n\n    # We only edit as many paragraphs as we have items; if item count differs, adjust.\n        # For this task data, it is expected to be 3.\n        parent_map = {c: p for p in root.iter() for c in list(p)}\n        first_parent = parent_map.get(followup_ps[0])\n\n        if first_parent is None:\n            raise RuntimeError(\"Could not locate parent for follow-up paragraphs\")\n\n        # If counts differ, remove extras / insert clones.\n        existing = followup_ps\n        desired_n = len(followup_items)\n\n        # Remove extra paragraphs\n        if len(existing) > desired_n:\n            for extra in existing[desired_n:]:\n                first_parent.remove(extra)\n            existing = existing[:desired_n]\n\n        # Add missing paragraphs by cloning last existing\n        if len(existing) < desired_n:\n            if not existing:\n                raise RuntimeError(\"No existing follow-up paragraph to clone\")\n            template = existing[-1]\n            insert_after = template\n            for _i in range(desired_n - len(existing)):\n                clone = copy.deepcopy(template)\n                #
